Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the tissue space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 152 and the convergence rate is O(260/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_152(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 152. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^260(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The tissue variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 830 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 158 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to tissue theory, we decompose the problem into 830 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 158. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 214.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: For bound derivation pairs (A,B) in tissue theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 299 and the invariant I(A,B) = 513 mod 2.

Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
